Conclusion: 超导铜氧化物/过渡金属硫族化物异质结构是工程化交错磁并研究其与非常规超导耦合的有前景平台。

Abstract: Realizing altermagnetism in high-$T_c$ cuprate-based systems would provide a direct route for studying spin-split electronic bands in the absence of net magnetization and investigate their interplay with unconventional superconductivity. Here, we propose that FeSe/cuprate heterostructures offer such a platform, where a 45$^\circ$ twist of Cu and Fe layers creates an effective CuFe$_2$ Lieb lattice in which Fe magnetic order and Cu-Fe hybridization through the ligands induces altermagnetic $d$-wave spin splitting. A minimal tight-binding model shows that this mechanism is generic. Furthermore, a substrate-induced inequivalence of the two Se sites in FeSe provides a second route in which altermagnetism originates in the Fe layer and is transferred to the cuprate layer by proximity. Density functional theory calculations for FeSe/Bi$_2$Sr$_2$CuO$_6$ heterostructures confirm the viability of both mechanisms and reveal ways to enhance the spin splitting. These results establish superconducting cuprate/transition metal chalcogenide heterostructures as a promising setting for engineering altermagnetism and studying its coupling to unconventional superconductivity.

</details>

Motivation: 探索通过光场（Floquet工程）调控三维拓扑绝缘体表面态能带结构，实现纯排斥相互作用下的超导电性，为无传统声子媒介的超导机制提供新的研究平台。

Method: 利用圆偏振光驱动三维拓扑绝缘体表面态，通过Floquet工程减小甚至翻转表面态色散的内在曲率，实现近平坦带或墨西哥帽型能带；结合金属栅极屏蔽作用，考察纯排斥库仑相互作用下是否支持拓扑超导。

Result: 在标准三维拓扑绝缘体参数下，合理电场强度即可达到平坦带极限；改变驱动场强度可实现与菱方石墨烯在变化位移场下相似的能带结构。配合适当放置的金属栅极，在低电子密度（10^11-10^12/cm^2）范围内可获得约7K的超导转变温度，同时避免维格纳结晶。

Conclusion: 圆偏振光驱动的Floquet工程为实现拓扑超导提供了一种灵活可控的新方案，该方案无需传统声子媒介，仅依赖排斥性库仑相互作用即可获得可观转变温度的超导态。

Abstract: We demonstrate that circularly polarized light can be used to Floquet-engineer nearly flat or Mexican-hat like electronic bands on the surface of three-dimensional topological insulators (3D TIs), which under suitable conditions, can support topological superconductivity via purely repulsive Coulomb interactions. The driving acts not merely by gapping out the Dirac cone on the surface of the 3D TI, but can be used to diminish, and even flip in sign, the intrinsic curvature of the surface state dispersion away from the Dirac point. Using parameters for canonical 3D TIs, we find that the flat band limit is attained for reasonable electric fields and the bands realized by changing the strength of the driving field have a similar energetic and spatial profile to those obtained in rhombohedral graphene under varying displacement field, where the case for superconductivity with purely repulsive interactions has recently been made. We find that, with the aid of appropriately placed screening metallic gate, one can obtain $T_c \sim 7 $K in this setup while avoiding Wigner crystallization for low electron densities in the range of $10^{11}-10^{12}/\text{cm}^2$.

### [92] [Excited state optimization for strongly correlated quantum defects using ensemble variational Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27377)
*Kevin G. Kleiner,Lucas K. Wagner*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文利用最近提出的系综变分蒙特卡洛（ensemble VMC）方法，研究了强关联点缺陷（包括金刚石中的氮-空位和硅-空位中心，以及氮化铝中的替代铁和铬杂质）的优化波函数。研究发现PBE0杂化泛函轨道优于PBE半局域轨道（可导致激发能变化高达0.5 eV），直接优化目标泛函可进一步改进（激发能变化达0.2 eV），且各参数的重要性因缺陷而异，凸显了全面优化所有参数的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强关联点缺陷（如色心）对量子技术应用至关重要，但其激发态的准确描述极具挑战。传统方法难以同时优化波函数中的行列式展开参数、轨道和Jastrow关联因子，论文旨在通过系综VMC方法实现对所有这些参数的全面优化，以获得更精确的激发态能量。

Method: 采用系综变分蒙特卡洛（ensemble VMC）方法，对多个强关联点缺陷体系（金刚石中的NV中心和SiV中心，AlN中的Fe和Cr杂质）进行波函数优化。全面优化三个参数类别：行列式展开参数、单粒子轨道（对比PBE和PBE0杂化泛函初始轨道）以及Jastrow关联因子，并系统评估各参数对目标泛函和激发能的影响。

Result: PBE0杂化泛函轨道作为初始轨道明显优于PBE半局域轨道（目标泛函值更低），激发能变化最高达0.5 eV；进一步直接优化目标泛函后，激发能可再改变达0.2 eV。每个缺陷体系中起主导作用的优化参数各不相同，说明不同缺陷的最优参数组合具有特异性。

Conclusion: 要获得强关联点缺陷的准确激发态，必须对所有波函数参数（行列式展开、轨道和Jastrow因子）进行全面优化，而非仅依赖某一种参数优化。系综VMC方法为这类体系的精确激发态计算提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Using the recently introduced ensemble variational Monte Carlo (VMC), we study optimized wave functions for strongly correlated point defects, including nitrogen-vacancy and silicon-vacancy centers in diamond and substitutional iron and chromium impurities in aluminum nitride. We study the effects of fully optimized determinant expansion parameters, orbitals, and Jastrow correlation factors on these systems. We find that orbitals from the hybrid functional PBE0 are much better (have lower objective functional) than semilocal PBE, which results in changes in the excitation energies up to 0.5 eV. Further improvements can be made by directly optimizing the objective functional, resulting in changes in excitation energies up to 0.2 eV. The most important parameter varies from defect to defect, reinforcing the necessity of optimizing all parameters to obtain accurate excited states in strongly correlated defects.

</details>


### [93] [Universality of Energy-Space Entanglement in Quantum Impurity Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27394)
*Geng-Dong Zhou,Zhi-Da Song*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子杂质模型中能量空间二分的纠缠熵（EE）。通过对浴进行对数离散化并划分为高、低能扇区，发现费米液体不动点的低能EE流动到与模型参数无关的常数（为ln2的整数倍加上仅依赖离散化参数的修正）。通过将能量空间尺度不变性映射为有效一维链的平移不变性，不动点可用一维拓扑带理论分类，每个ln2贡献源于拓扑边缘模。此外，在局域单态-近藤单态转变中，EE的差异对应有效浴链的拓扑转变；非费米液体临界点的EE不稳定平台具有与欠屏蔽双通道近藤模型类似的Λ依赖，支持同一普适类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上纠缠熵研究基于实空间二分，但作者提出能量空间（等价于浴的动量空间）二分在量子杂质模型中也可能展现普适行为。这一新视角有望揭示杂质模型不动点的更深层结构，并建立与拓扑带理论的联系。

Method: 作者借鉴poor man's scaling的动机，对浴进行对数离散化并分为高、低能扇区，计算能量空间的纠缠熵。对于费米液体不动点（Anderson模型、完全屏蔽和欠屏蔽近藤模型），分析低能EE的流动行为。利用能量空间尺度不变性映射到一维链的有效平移不变性，采用一维拓扑带理论对不动点进行分类。在局域单态-近藤单态转变（双轨道Anderson模型，由轨道间反铁磁耦合驱动）中研究不同费米液体不动点之间的转变，并分析非费米液体临界点的EE平台及其Λ依赖。

Result: 费米液体不动点的低能EE流动到模型参数无关的常数，为整数倍的ln2加上仅依赖于对数离散化参数Λ的修正。在低能手征对称性下，每个ln2贡献对应一个拓扑边缘模。局域单态相具有可忽略的EE，而近藤单态相具有超过每自旋每轨道ln2的有限EE；在低能手征对称性下，这对应有效浴链的拓扑转变。非费米液体临界点处EE出现不稳定平台，其Λ依赖与欠屏蔽双通道近藤模型相似，表明属于同一非费米液体普适类。

Conclusion: 能量空间二分产生的纠缠熵在量子杂质模型中展现出丰富的普适行为，建立了费米液体与非费米液体不动点与一维拓扑带理论之间的深刻联系，为量子杂质系统的分类提供了新的手段。

Abstract: Entanglement entropy (EE) is commonly studied using real-space bipartitions. We show that, in quantum impurity models, an energy-space bipartition, equivalent to the momentum-space bipartition of the bath, can display universal behavior. Motivated by poor man's scaling, we logarithmically discretize the bath and partition it into high- and low-energy sectors. For models with Fermi-liquid fixed points, including the Anderson model and fully screened or underscreened Kondo models, the low-energy EE flows to constants independent of model parameters. These constants are integer multiples of $\ln 2$ plus corrections that depend only on the logarithmic discretization parameter $Λ$. We show that scale invariance of the fixed-point wavefunction in energy space maps to effective translation invariance along a one-dimensional chain, allowing the fixed points to be classified by one-dimensional topological band theory. With low-energy chiral symmetry, each $\ln 2$ contribution originates from a topological edge mode. We also study transitions between distinct Fermi-liquid fixed points using the local-singlet--Kondo-singlet transition in a two-orbital Anderson model driven by an inter-orbital antiferromagnetic coupling. The local-singlet phase has an effectively decoupled impurity and nearly vanishing EE, whereas the Kondo-singlet phase has finite EE larger than $\ln 2$ per spin and orbital. When chiral symmetry holds at low energies, this distinction corresponds to a topological transition of the effective bath chain. At the non-Fermi-liquid critical point, the EE develops an unstable plateau. Its $Λ$ dependence resembles that of the overscreened two-channel Kondo model, supporting universality within the same non-Fermi-liquid universality class.

</details>


### [94] [Iterative minimization in reduced density matrix functional theory for periodic systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27679)
*Kai Luo,Jingang Han,Peize Lin,Daye Zheng,Mohan Chen,Xinguo Ren*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基组无关的约化密度矩阵泛函理论（RDMFT）公式，并实现了用于周期性固体的平面波实现。通过自适应压缩交换机制评估非局域交换关联泛函，自然占用数用谱投影梯度（SPG）或一阶隐式-显式（EBI）映射优化，自然轨道通过复Stiefel流形上的黎曼优化更新。在H2、硅和钠等典型体系上的基准测试表明SPG方法更稳定且能复现收敛的杂化参考结果，而EBI在占用数接近0或1时可能停滞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RDMFT为强关联体系提供了超越Kohn-Sham密度泛函理论的途径，但到目前为止，对周期性固体的实际计算仍然难以实现。作者旨在填补这一空白，开发适用于扩展体系的RDMFT计算方法。

Method: 采用基组无关的方式表述周期体系的RDMFT，结合平面波基组实现迭代最小化；利用自适应压缩交换机制评估非局域交换关联泛函；自然占用数通过谱投影梯度（SPG）方法或一阶隐式-显式（EBI）映射在N可表示性约束下优化；自然轨道通过复Stiefel流形上的黎曼优化更新。

Result: 在H2、硅和钠体系上使用Hartree-Fock泛函的基准测试表明，SPG能复现收敛的杂化参考结果，而EBI在占用数接近0或1时会停滞。使用Power和Müller泛函时，SPG比EBI获得更低的能量和更稳定的收敛。对带分数电荷的LiH、解离的H2和N2分子以及硅的状态方程的应用表明算法可靠且稳健。

Conclusion: 所提出的平面波RDMFT实现是可靠且稳健的，SPG方法在周期性固体的RDMFT计算中优于EBI方法，为强关联周期体系的电子结构计算提供了实用的工具。

Abstract: Reduced density matrix functional theory (RDMFT) offers a route beyond Kohn-Sham density functional theory for strongly correlated systems, yet practical calculations for periodic solids are still out of reach. We formulate RDMFT for extended systems in a basis-independent way and present a planewave implementation using iterative minimization for periodic solids, evaluating nonlocal exchange-correlation functionals through the existing adaptive compressed exchange machinery. Natural occupations are optimized under N-representability constraints with a spectral projected gradient (SPG) method or an first-order explicit-by-implicit (EBI) map, while natural orbitals are updated by Riemannian optimization on the complex Stiefel manifolds. Benchmarks on typical systems of \ce{H2}, silicon, and sodium with the Hartree-Fock functional show that SPG reproduces converged hybrid references, whereas EBI can stall when occupations approach $0$ or $1$. With the power and Müller functionals, SPG yields lower energies and more stable convergence than EBI. Applications to fractionally charged \ce{LiH}, dissociating \ce{H2} and \ce{N2} molecules, and equation of state of silicon show that the algorithm presented in this implementation is reliable and robust.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定强关联电子系统中电荷载流子的本质是核心目标。在扭曲双层石墨烯中，虽然基态是带隙绝缘体，但理解其有限温度正常态中的激发性质对揭示强关联拓扑带的物性至关重要。

Method: 采用连续场动量空间量子蒙特卡洛（continuous-field momentum-space QMC）这一无偏的量子多体计算方法，在投影的相关平坦带模型中研究扭曲双层石墨烯的有限温度性质。

Result: 发现温度虽然远低于相互作用能标（约3 meV vs 相互作用尺度），系统已进入强涨落的对称正常态。该态中存在由两个电子和一个空穴构成的三粒子束缚态（Dirac trion），在零动量无间隙点处与高能电子严格正交；这些Dirac trion有效质量极轻（尽管由重载流子组成），且其能谱可通过扭转角和层间跃迁强度方便地调控。

Abstract: Determining the nature of charge carriers is a fundamental goal in the study of strongly correlated electron systems. Here, we employ the continuous-field momentum-space quantum Monte Carlo method to reveal exotic "Dirac trion" excitations in the finite-temperature normal state of twisted bilayer graphene. While the ground state is a symmetry-breaking insulator with gapped ($\sim$ 20 meV) electron-like excitations, we show that a small temperature ($\sim$ 3 meV), well below the interaction scale, drives the system into a strongly fluctuating symmetric normal state. We demonstrate that this normal state hosts gapless excitations consisting of three-particle bound states, two electrons and one hole, that are exactly orthogonal to the higher-energy electrons at the zero-momentum gapless point. These Dirac trions have the remarkable property of being arbitrarily light despite being composed of heavy constituents, and their spectra can be easily tuned by varying the twist angle and interlayer hopping strength. Our unbiased quantum many-body computation sheds light on the Dirac trions in a projected correlated flat-band setting and opens the door for further investigation of many-body excitations in strongly correlated topological bands beyond Landau levels.

</details>


### [96] [Finite-size effects and interaction-driven crossovers in quarter-filled attractive Hubbard model: Exact diagonalization, DMRG and machine-learning analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27916)
*Md Fahad Equbal,Satoru Hayami*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观测量对相互作用驱动的关联性重组的表征可能依赖特定序参量选择，缺乏无偏的系统性方法。本文旨在利用无监督机器学习技术对多体关联矩阵进行无偏分析，从而独立且客观地刻画从弱关联费米子到强配对态的交叉现象，并验证其有限尺寸标度行为。

Method: 采用三种互补的方法：(1) 精确对角化和密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）计算基态能量学、局域观测量及关联函数；(2) 计算两孔和三孔束缚能以提供配对形成的能量证据；(3) 对实空间关联矩阵应用主成分分析（PCA）和均匀流形逼近与投影（UMAP）两种无监督机器学习技术，进行无偏的关联景观表征。

Conclusion: 无监督机器学习方法（PCA和UMAP）对实空间关联矩阵的分析提供了序参量独立的关联重组表征能力，能够独立复现由传统观测量推断出的BCS-BEC型交叉现象，且该特征在有限尺寸标度下保持稳定，验证了这些机器学习方法作为多体量子系统无偏分析工具的有效性。

Abstract: We investigate the quarter-filled attractive Hubbard model on finite-width cylindrical lattices using exact diagonalization (ED), density-matrix renormalization group (DMRG) and unsupervised machine-learning-based techniques. Analysis of the ground-state energetics, local observables and correlation functions reveals a continuous interaction-driven crossover from weakly correlated fermions to a regime dominated by tightly bound singlet pairs. This crossover originates from the competition between kinetic-energy-driven fermionic itinerancy and interaction-driven onsite pair formation and exhibits behavior consistent with the BCS--BEC crossover in the thermodynamic limit. Hole-binding-energy calculations provide direct energetic evidence for pair formation: the two-hole binding energy remains negative throughout the attractive regime whereas three-hole binding emerges only at sufficiently strong attraction and exhibits pronounced finite-size dependence. To obtain an unbiased characterization of the correlation landscape, we apply principal component analysis (PCA) and uniform manifold approximation and projection (UMAP) to the real-space correlation matrices. PCA reveals a systematic redistribution of correlation variance whereas UMAP identifies a clear separation between weak- and strong-pairing regimes. Both machine-learning-based approaches independently identify the same crossover region inferred from conventional observables while providing an order-parameter-independent characterization of the underlying reorganization of many-body correlations. Finite-size scaling analyses of the pairing structure factor and the leading PCA variance ratio demonstrate that these signatures remain robust with increasing system size.

</details>

Abstract: Altermagnet CsCr$_2$S$_2$O undergoes a Verwey-type metal-to-insulator transition (MIT) driven by lattice distortion and a stripe charge order on the Cr sublattice, reminiscent of the physics in Fe$_3$O$_4$. However, atomic distortions occur exclusively at the ligand sites rather than the Cr sites. What drives such a pronounced charge imbalance between Cr sites thus remains a mystery. Utilizing DFT+DMFT calculations, we identify an orbital-selective Mott transition, which leaves the correlated metallic $d_{yz}$ orbital governing the low-energy physics. We demonstrate that S-site distortions trigger an initial, tiny charge asymmetry between Cr sites via Cr-$d_{yz}$ and S-$p$ orbital hybridization. Crucially, this asymmetry is significantly amplified by dynamical electronic correlations, resulting in a large discrepancy in both the charge and electron correlations of the Cr-$d_{yz}$ orbital between distinct Cr sites. This further induces a substantial differentiation in local spin polarizations in the altermagnetic state, ultimately driving the MIT. In contrast, we predict that replacing S with Te weakens this correlation-amplification effect and fails to induce an MIT due to weaker electron correlations. Our findings demonstrate that many-body effects can drastically amplify ligand instabilities to reshape the electronic structure of altermagnets, highlighting that ligand engineering is of paramount importance for realizing robust metallic altermagnetism.

</details>

Method: 以金红石型易平面d波交错磁体（如NiF₂）为研究对象，分析其磁子色散和磁矩特性，推导交错磁产生的依赖于动量的磁子磁矩，并计算温度梯度下磁子驱动的自旋电流（自旋分裂效应），研究外磁场对热自旋电导率的调控。

Result: 证明了易平面体系中同样会出现交错磁产生的磁子磁矩，该磁矩依赖动量且具有d波对称性；由此产生自旋分裂效应，即温度梯度作用下出现磁子驱动的自旋电流；热自旋电导率可通过垂直易平面的外磁场有效调控。

Conclusion: 该工作将交错磁引发的热磁子分裂效应从易轴体系拓展到易平面体系，并揭示了外磁场作为调控自旋输运的有效手段，为交错磁体在自旋热电子学中的应用提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Altermagnets constitute a novel class of collinear spin-compensated materials in which magnon branches are spin-split even in the non-relativistic limit. The latter is the result of a more complex symmetry operation (compared to conventional antiferromagnets) that connects the two sublattices. Altermagnetic splitting strongly affects the magnon transport properties, leading, in particular, to the thermal magnon splitter effect in easy-axial $d$-wave altermagnets. Whether this effect also appears in easy-planar systems is not obvious, since the magnon branches do not carry a constant magnetic moment in this case. Here, we consider the easy-planar $d$-wave altermagnet of a rutile-type, e.g., NiF$_2$, and demonstrate the emergence of the altermagnetically generated magnon magnetic moment, which is momentum-dependent and possesses $d$-wave symmetry. This then leads to the spin-splitter effect, i.e., the emergence of a magnon-driven spin current (the flow of magnetic moment) in response to the applied temperature gradient. We also demonstrate that the corresponding thermal spin conductivity can be effectively tuned by an external magnetic field perpendicular to the easy plane.

</details>


### [99] [Coupled Spin-Density-Wave and Bond-Order Driven Metal-Insulator Transition in Altermagnetic CsCr$_2$S$_2$O](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28329)
*Chenchao Xu,Wansheng Bai,Guo-Xiang Zhi,Yi Liu,Xiaoqun Wang,Jianhui Dai,Chao Cao*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究在CsCr₂S₂O中识别出一种由键序（BO）耦合次级自旋密度波（sSDW）驱动的金属-绝缘体转变（MIT），该耦合由预先存在的C型反铁磁序（C-AFM）破缺时间反演对称性而得以实现。第一性原理计算揭示了轨道选择性物理：Cr-d_{yz}轨道形成局域磁矩并建立交变磁序，而Cr-d_{xz}轨道保持金属性并与S-p_z杂化，在位相互作用驱动巡游d_{xz}电子的次级SDW不稳定性，与Cr-d_{xz}-S-p_z成键序耦合，共同产生实验观察到的结构畸变、电荷不均化、局域Cr磁矩调制和带隙开启。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解金属-绝缘体转变（MIT）与结构相变之间的耦合机制是凝聚态物理的核心问题之一。本文旨在揭示一种新的轨道选择性机制，其中预先存在的交变磁序（altermagnetism）和电子关联协同驱动结构MIT，为理解这类材料中磁序、轨道自由度与结构相变之间的复杂关联提供微观图像。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算方法，结合轨道分辨电子结构分析和在位相互作用效应。具体而言：（1）识别Cr-d_{yz}与Cr-d_{xz}轨道的选择性行为；（2）分析C-AFM序破缺时间反演对称性如何促成SDW-BO耦合；（3）通过on-site相互作用增强巡游d_{xz}电子的次级SDW不稳定性；（4）建立sSDW-BO耦合序与实验观测的结构畸变、电荷不均化、磁矩调制和带隙开启之间的对应关系。

Result: 研究发现在CsCr₂S₂O中存在由键序耦合次级自旋密度波驱动的MIT。Cr-d_{yz}轨道形成局域磁矩并建立交变磁序，Cr-d_{xz}轨道保持金属性并与S-p_z杂化主导低能物理。在位相互作用增强了巡游d_{xz}电子的sSDW不稳定性，该sSDW与Cr-d_{xz}-S-p_z成键序耦合，同时产生结构畸变、电荷不均化、局域Cr磁矩调制和带隙开启等实验可观测效应。

Conclusion: 本文建立了轨道选择性机制：预先存在的交变磁序与电子关联协同驱动结构MIT。该机制阐明了磁序（时间反演对称性破缺）如何为SDW-BO耦合创造前提条件，并解释了实验中同时出现的多种序参量变化，为理解和预测具有类似轨道选择性特征的磁性绝缘体转变提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: A metal-insulator transition (MIT) driven by bond order (BO) coupled with a secondary spin-density wave (SDW) is identified in CsCr$_2$S$_2$O. Such coupling is enabled as a result of the broken time-reversal symmetry due to the pre-existing C-type antiferromagnetic (C-AFM) order. First-principles calculations reveal an orbital-selective physics that Cr-$d_{yz}$ orbitals form local moments and establish the altermagnetic order, while the Cr-$d_{xz}$ orbitals remain metallic and hybridize with S-$p_z$. Thus the low-energy physics is governed by the Cr-$d_{xz}$ and S-$p_z$ orbitals. On-site interactions then enhance a secondary SDW ($s$SDW) instability of the itinerant $d_{xz}$ electrons, which couples to the Cr-$d_{xz}$-S-$p_z$ bonding order. The resulting coupled $s$SDW-BO simultaneously produces experimentally observed structural distortion, charge disproportionation, local Cr-moment modulation, and gap opening. Our results establish an orbital-selective mechanism upon which pre-existing altermagnetism and electronic correlations cooperate to drive a structural MIT.

</details>


### [100] [Field-Selected Topological Buffering in a Disordered Skyrmion Crystal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28458)
*Wenyu Su,Nvsen Ma,Chen Cheng,Hong-Gang Luo*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文通过大规模模拟三角晶格手性磁体中的随机DM相互作用，揭示了磁场在斯格明子晶体无序化过程中作为控制旋钮的作用：高场下出现拓扑缓冲区间（包含Bragg玻璃态和斯格明子玻璃态），低场下该区间消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 淬灭无序可以破坏晶体序但又不立即摧毁其拓扑纹理的拓扑性，然而在斯格明子晶体中这两个过程之间的关系尚不清楚。

Method: 采用三角晶格手性磁体的随机DM相互作用模型进行大规模模拟，通过有限尺寸标度、空间关联、缺陷统计和自旋自关联等多重手段进行分析。

Result: 高场下，平移相干性在弱无序尺度即丧失，而六重键取向序幸存至更大无序强度，总拓扑荷在更大尺度保持锁定，形成拓扑缓冲区间，依次包含Bragg玻璃态和斯格明子玻璃态。低场下，键取向无序与拓扑重构几乎同时发生，斯格明子玻璃窗口消失。

Conclusion: 磁场可作为分离晶体无序化与拓扑荷丢失的控制参量，拓扑缓冲是拓扑纹理在结构无序介质中保持鲁棒性的机制。

Abstract: Quenched disorder can disrupt crystalline order without immediately destroying the topology of its constituent textures, but the relation between these processes in skyrmion crystals remains unclear. Using large-scale simulations of a triangular-lattice chiral magnet with random DM interactions, we show that the magnetic field selects between two disordering routes. At high fields, global translational coherence is lost at a weak-disorder scale, while sixfold bond-orientational order survives to a larger disorder strength and the total topological charge remains nearly locked up to a substantially larger scale. The resulting interval defines a topological buffer containing a Bragg-glass- like skyrmion regime followed by a skyrmion-glass regime. Finite-size scaling, spatial correlations, defect statistics, and spin autocorrelations support their distinct structural and glassy character. At lower fields, bond-orientational disordering nearly coincides with topological reconstruction, eliminating the skyrmion-glass window and contracting the buffer. These results identify the magnetic field as a control knob for separating crystalline disordering from topological-charge loss and establish topological buffering as a mechanism by which topological textures can remain robust in structurally disordered media.

</details>


### [101] [Lattice composite Fermi liquid with broken inversion symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28613)
*Pavel A. Nosov,Zhengyan Darius Shi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了半填充Chern带中打破反演对称性的晶格复合费米液体的输运性质。研究发现，在零波矢下，反演对称性破缺使规范场涨落产生非解析的纵向光频电阻率（~|ω|^{4/3}）；在有限波矢下，产生非互易输运和Hall电导率随|q|的非解析依赖。此外还提出一种新的奇异直流输运机制：复合费米面上2k_F散射的重整化增强Umklapp弛豫，导致电阻率的非解析温度依赖。这些输运特征在连续量子霍尔体系中不存在，可在扭曲MoTe₂和菱面体石墨烯实验中被检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在连续量子霍尔体系中，复合费米液体的奇异动力学响应通常被隐藏，而晶格复合费米液体（在半填充Chern带中实现）由于晶格对称性降低，有望揭示这些隐藏的内在奇异输运行为。作者旨在识别晶格复合费米液体区别于连续量子霍尔对应物的独特输运签名，为近期在扭曲MoTe₂和菱面体石墨烯中观察到的零场复合费米液体提供可检验的理论预测。

Method: 本文采用分析理论方法，基于复合费米液体理论框架：1）在零波矢极限下，计算反演对称性破缺对规范场涨落-复合费米子耦合的影响，得到纵向光频电阻率的频率标度律；2）在有限波矢下分析非互易输运和非解析Hall电导率，探讨表面声波探测方案；3）提出复合费米面上2k_F散射重整化增强Umklapp弛豫的机制，分析其对直流电阻率温度依赖的影响。

Result: 在栅极屏蔽库仑相互作用下，纵向光频电阻率呈现Re ρ^{xx}(ω) ~ |ω|^{4/3}的非解析频率依赖；有限波矢下Hall电导率呈现~|q|的非解析依赖，并伴随非互易输运；此外2k_F散射重整化使直流电阻率出现非解析温度依赖。这些特征均可通过表面声波传播和直流输运实验直接探测。

Conclusion: 晶格复合费米液体因其降低的晶格对称性而展现出多个在连续量子霍尔对应物中不存在的奇异输运签名（包括非解析光频电阻率、非互易输运、非解析Hall电导率和非解析温度依赖的电阻率），这些签名可在扭曲MoTe₂和菱面体石墨烯的零场复合费米液体实验中得到直接验证。

Abstract: We study transport in lattice composite Fermi liquids realized in half-filled Chern bands with broken inversion symmetry. We show that reduced crystalline symmetry exposes intrinsic singular dynamical responses of composite fermions that are otherwise hidden in the conventional Landau-level setting. At zero wave vector, inversion breaking allows gauge-field fluctuations to generate a non-analytic longitudinal optical resistivity, with $\operatorname{Re}ρ^{xx}(ω)\sim |ω|^{4/3}$ for gate-screened Coulomb interactions. At finite wave vector $\mathbf{q}$, inversion breaking leads to nonreciprocal transport and a non-analytic $\sim |\mathbf{q}|$ dependence of the Hall conductivity, both of which can be probed through surface acoustic wave propagation. We also discuss a distinct mechanism for singular DC transport in lattice composite Fermi liquids: renormalization of $2k_F$ scattering at the composite Fermi surface enhances Umklapp relaxation and can lead to a non-analytic temperature dependence of the resistivity. Taken together, our results identify transport signatures of lattice composite Fermi liquids that are absent in their continuum quantum Hall counterparts and can be directly tested in ongoing experiments on twisted MoTe$_2$ and rhombohedral graphene, where evidence for zero-field composite Fermi liquids has recently been reported.

</details>


### [102] [From Exact Diagonalization to DMRG: A Complete Numerical Study of the Transverse-Field Ising Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28471)
*Chandra Sekhar Prayaga*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文对一维横向场Ising模型（TFIM）进行了系统数值研究，从L=8的小系统精确对角化到L=100的DMRG计算，采用统一方法论（相同场网格、相同收敛判据），记录并解决了早前数据中由键维收敛伪影导致的虚假不连续问题，并通过两种互补路径提取中心荷c_eff≈0.51-0.52，与精确二维Ising值c=1/2一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望在量子临界系统中建立一套严谨、可复现的有限尺寸数值分析范式，同时通过这个经典模型展示如何识别和消除数值收敛伪影（如键维收敛伪影导致的虚假不连续），并为从业者提供一个方法论上的警示案例。

Method: 使用统一方法论：相同磁场网格、在每个点独立运行chi=100和chi=200的DMRG作为收敛诊断，覆盖L=8,14,20的精确对角化至L=100的DMRG共七个系统尺寸。通过两条路径提取中心荷：一是链中熵的领头对数有限尺寸标度，二是同时应用于所有系统和所有键的完整Calabrese-Cardy公式，并系统排除短距离晶格修正。

Result: 获得了c_eff=0.51-0.52，与二维Ising普适类的精确中心荷c=1/2一致；明确展示了精确对角化与DMRG必须精确一致（而非近似一致）的区域；记录并解析了一个之前数据集中的键维收敛伪影所导致的虚假不连续。

Conclusion: 该工作既是一篇物理结果论文，也是一篇关于精细有限尺寸数值方法的工作性范例，强调了统一方法、严格的收敛诊断和系统排除晶格修正对于可靠提取普适量（如中心荷）的重要性。

Abstract: We present a self-contained numerical study of the one-dimensional transverse-field Ising model (TFIM), tracing its ground-state entanglement structure from exact diagonalization at small system size (L=8,14,20) through density-matrix renormalization group (DMRG) calculations up to L=100. A single, consistent methodology - identical field grid, identical convergence diagnostic (independent runs at bond dimensions chi=100 and chi=200 at every point) - is used across all seven system sizes, and we show explicitly where exact diagonalization and DMRG must agree exactly rather than merely approximately. Along the way we document and resolve a bond-dimension convergence artifact that produced a spurious discontinuity in an earlier, less systematic dataset, as a worked cautionary example for practitioners. Using the resolved dataset we extract the central charge of the transition via two complementary routes - the leading logarithmic finite-size scaling of the mid-chain entropy, and the full Calabrese-Cardy formula applied to every bond of every system size simultaneously - obtaining c_eff to 0.51-0.52 as short-distance lattice corrections are systematically excluded, consistent with the exact two-dimensional Ising value c=1/2. We review the quantum-classical (Suzuki-Trotter) correspondence that underlies this agreement and discuss how the Widom-Kadanoff scaling hypothesis transplants from the classical to the quantum problem. The manuscript is intended as both a physics result and a worked methodological example of careful finite-size numerics.

</details>


### [103] [Graph Neural Network Force Fields for Spin Dynamics in Metallic Magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28537)
*Ali Rayat,Yunhao Fan,Gia-Wei Chern*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图神经网络（GNN）的磁性力场框架，直接从电子结构计算中学习巡游磁性系统的有效磁能泛函，实现高效的自旋力矩评估，并准确复现了电子模拟所得的非平衡自旋动力学，为大规模非平衡磁性模拟提供了途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金属磁性体表现出由电子生成相互作用控制的复杂自旋动力学，其预测模拟通常需要在时间演化过程中反复求解底层电子问题，造成巨大的计算瓶颈。因此需要一种能够绕过重复电子结构计算的高效方法。

Method: 引入图神经网络（GNN）磁性力场框架，直接从头电子计算中学习巡游自旋动力学所依赖的有效磁能泛函。该方法在概念上类似于机器学习原子间势（machine-learned interatomic potentials），能够高效评估自旋力矩，同时捕捉巡游电子产生的非线性和空间扩展相互作用。

Result: 在具有共线、非共线和非共面磁序的代表性金属磁性体系上进行了基准测试。学习到的力场准确复现了电子生成的自旋力矩，得到的非平衡自旋动力学与直接电子模拟结果高度一致。

Conclusion: 图神经网络被证明是机器学习磁性力场的强大框架，为跨多长度和时间尺度的大规模非平衡磁性预测模拟提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Metallic magnets exhibit complex spin dynamics governed by electronically generated interactions. Predictive simulations of such dynamics typically require repeated solutions of an underlying electronic problem throughout the time evolution, creating a major computational bottleneck. Here we introduce a graph neural network (GNN) magnetic force-field framework that learns the effective magnetic energy functional governing itinerant spin dynamics directly from electronic calculations. Conceptually analogous to machine-learned interatomic potentials, the proposed framework enables efficient evaluation of spin torques while capturing the nonlinear and spatially extended interactions generated by itinerant electrons. We benchmark the method on representative metallic magnetic systems exhibiting collinear, noncollinear, and noncoplanar magnetic order. The learned force fields accurately reproduce electronically generated spin torques and yield nonequilibrium spin dynamics in excellent agreement with direct electronic simulations. Our results establish graph neural networks as a powerful framework for machine-learned magnetic force fields, providing a pathway toward predictive large-scale simulations of nonequilibrium magnetism across multiple length and time scales.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.dis-nn'></div>

### [104] [Semi-supervised Hopfield model: Theoretical and Numerical results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28173)
*Linda Albanese,Andrea Ladiana,Andrea Lepre*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文首次为Hopfield网络的半监督Hebbian学习建立了统计力学理论。通过将监督和无监督Hebbian核的凸组合作为突触耦合，利用信号-噪声分析推导一步Mattis磁化和学习阈值，并借助Guerra插值在高温存储区得到副本对称淬火压力。研究发现混合策略优于纯监督或纯无监督协议，但淬火压力在λ上为凸函数，因此λ是学习超参数而非热力学可选择的内部态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器学习实践中，完全标注数据是奢侈品，标注需要昂贵的人力，而未标注数据可以自动批量获取。半监督学习是应对这种不对称性的标准方法，但半监督Hebbian学习的统计力学理论仍然缺失。作者希望填补这一空白，从理论层面理解半监督Hebbian学习的基本计算能力。

Method: (1) 将突触耦合定义为监督和无监督Hebbian核的凸组合，加权参数为λ∈[0,1]；(2) 使用信号-噪声分析求解一步Mattis磁化和学习阈值；(3) 利用Guerra插值方法在高温存储区推导Replica Symmetric淬火压力，通过特特征通道分解处理监督和无监督通道产生的关联无序；(4) 证明淬火压力在λ上的凸性；(5) 通过Monte Carlo模拟验证所有分析结果。

Result: 相图显示混合策略优于纯监督和纯无监督两种协议。证明淬火压力在λ上是凸的，因此热力学无法选择内部混合态，λ应被视为学习超参数。所有解析发现均通过广泛的Monte Carlo模拟得到验证。

Conclusion: 该工作为半监督Hebbian学习建立了严格的统计力学框架，证明了混合监督与无监督策略在Hopfield网络中的优越性，并阐明了混合参数λ作为学习超参数的数学理论基础。

Abstract: In the daily practice of Machine Learning, fully labeled datasets are a luxury: labels demand expensive and time-consuming human annotation, whereas raw, unlabeled data can be harvested automatically and in bulk. Semi-supervised learning, where the network jointly exploits the few labeled and the many unlabeled examples at its disposal, is the standard answer to this asymmetry, yet a statistical mechanical theory of semi-supervised Hebbian learning is still lacking. In this paper we fill this gap for the Hopfield network: we prescribe a synaptic coupling given by the convex combination, weighted by a mixing parameter λin [0,1], of the supervised and unsupervised Hebbian kernels built from the same archetypes, and we solve for the emergent computational capabilities of the resulting network. A signal-to-noise analysis yields the one-step Mattis magnetization and the learning threshold, i.e. the minimum dataset size for stable retrieval. Using Guerra's interpolation, we then derive the Replica Symmetric quenched pressure in the high-storage regime, treating the correlated disorder generated by the supervised and unsupervised channels through a particular eigen-channel decomposition. The resulting phase diagram shows that a mixed strategy outperforms both pure protocols. Finally, we prove that the quenched pressure is convex in λ, so thermodynamics cannot select an interior mixture: λis therefore a learning hyperparameter. All the analytical findings are successfully checked against extensive Monte Carlo simulations.

</details>

### [105] [Harnessing Native Chromium Oxidation for Giant Orbital Torque and Field-Free Magnetization Switching in NiFe/Cr Bilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27306)
*Chirag Kalouni,Abhishek Kumar,Manish Kumar Mohanta,Kanupriya,Athira Ravindran K,Damanpreet Kaur,Preet Kamal,Puru Jena,Debangsu Roy*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文发现Cr的天然氧化物（CrOx）可将NiFe/Cr双层结构转变为无需转换层的自足双通道轨道电流源，其阻尼类扭矩效率达3.9×10^6 Ω^-1 m^-1，超过Pt（Ta）一个（两个）数量级，并实现1.58×10^11 A m^-2的无场磁化翻转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨道电流可超越传统重金属自旋霍尔源的电荷-自旋转换效率极限，但以往利用轨道电流需要厚的轨道霍尔材料或额外的重金属转换层。本工作的动机是探索一种无需转换层的自足式轨道电流源方案，并克服传统设计中效率受限的问题。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算预测表面氧化对轨道霍尔电导率的增强作用；实验上制备自然氧化的NiFe/Cr异质结构进行扭矩测量；提出包含氧化门控界面源的漂移-扩散模型，定量复现实验数据并提取界面轨道电流生成和输运参数。

Result: 第一性原理计算预测表面氧化的轨道霍尔电导率提升近三倍，源于Cr(3d)-O(2p)杂化。实验测得阻尼类扭矩效率3.9×10^6 Ω^-1 m^-1，超过Pt一个数量级、Ta两个数量级。Cr-CrOx界面生成轨道电流比体轨道霍尔通道强一个数量级以上，轨道输运长度约4 nm。无场磁化翻转电流密度低至1.58×10^11 A m^-2，优于重金属和CuOx基准。

Conclusion: 天然氧化可作为一种可扩展的策略来实现高效的轨道扭矩器件，确立Cr-CrOx界面作为强轨道电流源，并揭示氧化工程在轨道电子学中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Orbital currents offer charge-to-spin conversion beyond the efficiency limit of conventional heavy-metal Spin Hall sources. However, harnessing them has so far required either thick orbital-Hall materials or additional heavy-metal conversion layers. Here, we show that the native oxide of chromium, typically regarded as parasitic, transforms a simple NiFe\Cr bilayer into a self-contained dual-channel orbital-current source without the need for any conversion layer. First-principles calculations predict a nearly threefold enhancement of the orbital Hall conductivity upon surface oxygenation, driven by Cr(3d)-O(2p) hybridization. Experimentally, naturally oxidized NiFe\Cr heterostructures exhibit a giant damping-like torque efficiency of $3.9 \times 10^{6}$ $Ω^{-1}$ m$^{-1}$, exceeding Pt (Ta) by one (two) orders of magnitude. The torque depicts a non-monotonic Cr-thickness dependence which cannot be explained by a conventional model. We have developed a drift-diffusion model with an oxidation-gated interfacial source which quantitatively reproduces the data, revealing that the Cr-CrO$_x$ interface generates orbital currents over an order of magnitude stronger than the bulk orbital Hall channel with an orbital transport length of $\approx 4$ nm. The enhanced torque enables field-free magnetization switching at $1.58 \times 10^{11}$ A m$^{-2}$, outperforming heavy-metal and CuO$_x$ benchmarks. These results establish native oxidation as a scalable strategy for realizing efficient orbital-torque devices.

</details>


### [106] [Fluctuation electrodynamics of quantum capacitance in electron bilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27406)
*Dmitry Zverevich,Alberto F. Morpurgo,Alex Levchenko*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文发展了电子双层结构中量子电容的理论，证明层间分离相关的地态能量在环图级别上正是非推迟Lifshitz范德瓦尔斯能量表达式，层间修正电容为Casimir可压缩性。作者给出了双层等离激元对电容的闭式贡献（含普适系数），并用Gell-Mann-Brueckner分析得到精确高密度极限。石墨烯体系中，单层遵循电子气模板，而双层石墨烯因带间屏蔽异常：修正被压制数个量级并在实验相关间距范围内符号反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电容在低维系统中受交换、关联作用影响显著，而层间关联对电容的贡献（Casimir可压缩性）此前缺乏系统理论。作者旨在建立一个连接范德瓦尔斯/卡西米尔物理与电容测量的统一理论框架，并辨析不同材料（半导体量子阱、石墨烯）中层间关联的表现差异。

Method: 采用泛函积分形式，在环图（ring diagram）级别将分离相关的地态能映射为基于层极化率的非推迟Lifshitz范德瓦尔斯表达式，从而将逆电容的层间修正表达为基态能对密度的二阶导数（Casimir可压缩性）。进一步使用Gell-Mann-Brueckner（GMB）分析给出高密度极限的普适函数，并对单层/双层石墨烯分别建模计算。

Result: （1）层间关联对电容的贡献以闭式给出，且带普适系数；其符号表明高密度下关联倾向于抵抗充电。（2）GMB分析给出精确高密度极限。（3）单层石墨烯遵循电子气行为，但双层石墨烯因带间屏蔽效应，层间修正被压制数个量级，并在实验相关分离范围内符号反转。

Conclusion: 量子电容可作为探测层间关联（Casimir可压缩性）的有效热力学探针。在稀薄Wigner晶体区和电子-空穴双层接近激子凝聚的区间，该理论失效，此时电容转而成为层间配对（interlayer pairing）的探针，为未来实验提供了新方向。

Abstract: Capacitance is a thermodynamic probe of interacting electrons: it measures the energy cost of moving charge between conductors, and in low-dimensional systems this cost is shaped by exchange and correlation as much as by electrostatics. We develop a theory of the quantum capacitance of electron double layers, semiconductor quantum wells as well as monolayer and bilayer graphene devices, focusing on the contribution generated by interlayer correlations. Within a functional-integral formulation we show that the separation-dependent part of the ground-state energy is, at the level of ring diagrams, exactly the nonretarded Lifshitz expression for the van der Waals energy of two conducting sheets, with reflection amplitudes built from the layer polarizabilities. The interlayer correction to the inverse capacitance is the second density derivative of this energy: a Casimir compressibility. The zero-point fluctuations that generate Casimir forces between mirrors are here the coupled plasmons of the bilayer, and their contribution to the capacitance is obtained in closed form, with a universal coefficient; its sign shows that interlayer correlations oppose charging at high density. A Gell-Mann-Brueckner analysis gives the exact high-density limit in terms of universal functions. For graphene we find that monolayers follow the electron-gas template, while bilayer graphene is anomalous: interband screening suppresses the correction by orders of magnitude and reverses its sign in the experimentally relevant range of separations. We delineate the limits of the theory, identifying the dilute Wigner-crystal regime and electron-hole double layers near exciton condensation as regimes where the capacitance becomes a probe of interlayer pairing.

</details>


### [107] [Entropic signatures of the single-impurity Kondo state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27502)
*Johann Drayne,Silvia Lüscher,Will Grant,Vahid Movahed,Tim Child,Saeed Fallahi,Geoffrey C. Gardner,Michael J. Manfra,Yaakov Kleeorin,Yigal Meir,Joshua Folk*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文报道了通过电荷传感和麦克斯韦关系对GaAs量子点中Kondo单态形成伴随的熵抑制进行的热力学测量，揭示了dN/dT不对称线形及其峰值位移，并通过NRG计算定性复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kondo单态是局域自旋与费米海纠缠形成的多体态，以往主要通过量子点中的输运特征进行研究。作者旨在通过热力学手段直接测量Kondo单态形成过程中的熵抑制，从而补充输运测量的视角。

Method: 使用温度依赖的电荷感应技术，结合麦克斯韦关系，在强耦合GaAs量子点中加入第一个电子时追踪自旋熵的抑制过程。通过绘制dN/dT与同时测量的占据数N的关系图来分析线形，并与数值重整化群（NRG）计算进行比较。

Result: dN/dT呈现不对称线形，峰值向N>1/2方向移动，这是Kondo屏蔽的标志性特征；该特征随温度升高而减弱。NRG计算定性再现了该行为，但存在向低占据数方向的微小持续偏移。同一器件上独立的电导-占据数测量与NRG在混合价交叉区域吻合。

Conclusion: 研究成功通过热力学测量直接探测了Kondo单态形成过程中的熵抑制，验证了理论预期，并展示了热力学与输运测量在表征Kondo物理中的互补性。

Abstract: The Kondo singlet---a many-body state formed by entanglement between a localized spin and the Fermi sea---has been studied extensively through its transport signatures in quantum dots. Here we report a thermodynamic measurement of the entropy suppression associated with the formation of the Kondo singlet, using temperature-dependent charge sensing and a Maxwell relation to track the suppression of spin entropy as the first electron is added to a strongly-coupled GaAs quantum dot. Plotting $dN/dT$ against the simultaneously measured occupation $N$ reveals an asymmetric lineshape with its peak shifted to $N>1/2$---a hallmark of Kondo screening---that weakens with increasing temperature and is qualitatively reproduced by numerical renormalization group (NRG) calculations, with a small but persistent offset to lower occupation relative to the theory. An independent measurement of conductance versus occupation on the same device provides a test of these quantities through the mixed-valence crossover and matches NRG within experimental uncertainty.

</details>


### [108] [Third-order nonlinear transport in a percolative two-dimensional superconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27641)
*Wenjun Liu,Chenghe Wang,Xiubin Li,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Tao Zhang,Xiao-Xiao Zhang,Jing Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文报道了三层1T′-MoTe2超导体在渗流转变区内的显著三阶非线性输运现象，其三阶谐波纵向电压对激发电流呈现明确的立方依赖关系，并可通过含时Ginzburg-Landau理论中的超导涨落半定量解释，表明三阶非线性输运可作为渗流体系中超导转变的灵敏探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维范德瓦尔斯材料中渗流超导现象普遍存在，其维度降低、量子涨落增强以及复杂电子-声子相互作用为正常电子与库珀对共存提供了独特平台，但缺乏对该体系中超导转变的灵敏探测手段。

Method: 在三层1T′-MoTe2超导体的渗流转变区内测量其三阶谐波纵向电压V∥^(3ω)，分析其对激发电流的依赖关系，并结合含时Ginzburg-Landau理论对涨落库珀对产生的非线性输运进行半定量描述。

Result: 观察到三阶谐波纵向电压在阈值以下对激发电流呈明确的三次方依赖，其幅度和非线性系数与超导态强相关；该非线性响应可由含时Ginzburg-Landau理论中的超导涨落半定量捕获。

Conclusion: 三阶非线性输运可作为渗流体系中超导转变的灵敏探针，并为在强关联系统中探索高阶级输运现象奠定基础。

Abstract: Percolative superconductivity frequently arises in two-dimensional van der Waals materials due to reduced dimensionality, enhanced quantum fluctuations, and complex electron-phonon interactions, providing a unique platform where normal electrons coexist with Cooper pairs. We report the observation of substantial third-order nonlinear transport in a trilayer $1T^\prime$-MoTe$_2$ superconductor within its percolative transition regime. The third-harmonic longitudinal voltage ($V_{\|}^{3ω}$) exhibits a clear cubic dependence on excitation current below a threshold, with both its magnitude and nonlinear coefficient strongly correlated with the superconducting state. This nonlinear response is semiquantitatively captured by the superconducting fluctuation within the time-dependent Ginzburg-Landau theory, where nonlinear transport arises due to fluctuating Cooper pairs. Our results demonstrate that third-order nonlinear transport serves as a sensitive probe of superconducting transitions in percolative systems and establish a foundation for exploring higher-order transport phenomena in strongly correlated systems.

</details>


### [109] [Significant ordinary Nernst effect contribution to spin-orbit torque harmonic Hall measurements in metallic structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27813)
*Igor Lyalin,C. C. Chiang,C. L. Chien*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文发现Cr/Co双层结构中普通能斯特效应对二次谐波霍尔电压的贡献不可忽略，会导致自旋轨道转矩效率被显著高估。通过额外实验估算了Cr的能斯特系数，并指出以往使用谐波霍尔技术量化金属自旋轨道转矩的研究结果可能需要重新审视。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谐波霍尔测量是量化铁磁/正常金属双层结构中自旋轨道转矩的常用方法，但传统分析通常假设普通能斯特效应的贡献可忽略。本研究旨在验证这一假设在Cr/Co双层结构中是否成立，并评估其对自旋轨道转矩效率测量准确性的影响。

Method: 在Cr/Co双层结构中进行谐波霍尔测量，通过对比分析考虑与忽略普通能斯特效应时的数据结果；同时设计额外实验来单独估算Cr的能斯特系数，并与文献中其他金属的能斯特系数进行比较。

Result: 研究发现Cr/Co双层结构中普通能斯特效应贡献显著不可忽视，会导致自旋轨道转矩效率被大幅高估；Cr的能斯特系数与其他金属报道值相当，表明该效应在金属体系中具有普遍性，不应被简单忽略。

Conclusion: 在应用谐波霍尔技术量化金属中的自旋轨道转矩时，必须仔细考虑普通能斯特效应的贡献。本研究结果提示部分先前工作得到的自旋轨道转矩效率数据可能需要被重新审查和修正。

Abstract: The harmonic Hall measurements are commonly used to quantify the spin-orbit torque in ferromagnet/normal metal bilayers. The contribution from ordinary Nernst effect to the second harmonic voltages is usually assumed to be negligible and omitted in the data analysis. We show that in Cr/Co bilayers the ordinary Nernst effect cannot be neglected and can lead to largely exaggerated values of spin-orbit torque efficiency. Conducting additional experiments, we estimate the Nernst coefficient of Cr and find it comparable to the values reported for other metals. Consequently, the ordinary Nernst effect should be carefully considered when employing the harmonic Hall technique to quantify spin-orbit torque in metals, while the results of some previous works might need to be re-examined.

</details>


### [110] [Scalar-spin-chirality-driven fractional Chern insulator on a kagome lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27833)
*Shinnosuke Tsutsumi,Koji Kudo,Kentaro Nomura*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文在具有非共面磁序和标量自旋手性的kagome磁性材料中证明了分数陈绝缘体（FCI）态的出现，发现更强的电子-电子相互作用（相对于能带色散）能在更宽的标量自旋手性范围内稳定FCI态，并通过基态简并度、有限能隙和谱流等方式对其进行了表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FCI是分数量子霍尔态在晶格中的类比，无需外加磁场即可实现。现有研究多关注模型化体系，而kagome磁体中天然存在的非共面磁序可自发诱导标量自旋手性，为在真实材料中实现FCI提供了潜在平台，但其与电子相互作用和能带色散竞争的关系尚不明确。

Method: 构建包含电子-电子相互作用和能带色散效应的kagome磁体模型，通过计算与代表性态的波函数重叠度、在热力学极限下识别基态简并度和有限能隙、以及追踪多个磁通量子插入时的谱流来表征FCI态。

Result: 发现更强的相互作用（相对于能带色散）能够在更宽的标量自旋手性参数范围内稳定FCI态；FCI态通过基态简并度、有限能隙和谱流等多个判据得到确认。

Conclusion: kagome磁性材料中的标量自旋手性可作为实现FCI态的有效途径，为实验上探索FCI提供了一个有前景的材料平台。

Abstract: Fractional Chern insulators (FCIs) are the lattice analogs of the fractional quantum Hall states, emerging even without an external magnetic field. In this work, we demonstrate the emergence of the FCI states in a kagome magnet with a noncoplanar magnetic order that induces a finite scalar spin chirality. By incorporating in our model both electron-electron interactions and the effect of band dispersion, we find that stronger interactions relative to the band dispersion stabilize the FCI state over a broader range of scalar spin chirality. We characterize the emergent FCI state by calculating overlap with representative states, identifying the ground-state degeneracy and the finite energy gap in the thermodynamic limit, and tracking the spectral flow under multiple flux-quantum insertions. Our results suggest that kagome magnets with scalar spin chirality can be promising platforms for realizing FCIs.

</details>


### [111] [Giant nonlinear Hall effect in a Pt/ferrimagnetic insulator bilayer under Zeeman-exchange frustration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27848)
*Takayuki Shiino,Matteo Fettizio,Weronika Janus,Can Onur Avci*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文报道了在Al取代的铽铁石榴石（具有厚度方向组分梯度）体系中，塞曼-交换相互作用竞争产生的磁性挫折（frustration）可在相邻Pt层中诱导出巨大的非线性霍尔响应。在磁补偿点附近，场致自旋翻转转变伴随异常的高阶奇次谐波电压，三次、五次、七次谐波幅度与基波相当。场、温度及电流依赖关系共同表明焦耳热是该谐波响应的参数驱动机制，宏自旋链模拟进一步验证了这一机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 竞争磁相互作用可产生亚稳态或不稳态，使磁性体系对外部扰动高度敏感。作者旨在探索利用磁性挫折机制，将微弱热扰动转化为巨大的非线性电学响应，为补偿铁磁体中的非线性磁输运提供新途径。

Method: 实验上，制备了具有厚度方向组分梯度的Al取代铽铁石榴石，并在其上层沉积Pt层；在磁补偿点附近系统测量霍尔电压的奇次谐波响应（三、五、七次谐波），并系统表征磁场、温度和电流依赖关系。理论上，利用宏自旋链（macrospin-chain）模拟，验证电流诱导的热调制周期性切换界面Fe磁化在交换主导与塞曼主导状态之间，复现实验观测到的谐波信号。

Result: 在磁补偿附近，场致自旋翻转转变伴随显著的高阶奇次谐波电压，第三、五、七次谐波幅度达到与基波相当的幅度。通过场、温度及电流依赖关系确认焦耳热为谐波响应的参数驱动机制。宏自旋链模拟成功复现了实验观测的谐波信号，证实了热调制驱动的磁性状态周期切换机制。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了磁性挫折如何将微弱热扰动转化为大幅度的非线性电学响应，揭示了补偿铁磁体中非线性磁输运的新物理机制，为基于磁性挫折的非线性磁电功能器件提供了设计思路。

Abstract: Competing magnetic interactions can create metastable or unstable states and render magnetic systems highly susceptible to external perturbations. Here we show that Zeeman-exchange frustration in an Al-substituted terbium iron garnet with a compositional gradient across its thickness gives rise to a giant nonlinear Hall response in an adjacent Pt layer. Near magnetic compensation, a field-induced spin-flip transition is accompanied by unusually large higher-order odd harmonic voltages, with the third, fifth, and seventh harmonics reaching amplitudes comparable to that of the first harmonic. The field, temperature, and current dependences collectively identify Joule heating as the parametric drive of the harmonic response. Macrospin-chain simulations further show that current-induced thermal modulation periodically switches the interfacial Fe magnetization between exchange- and Zeeman-dominated states and reproduces the observed harmonic signals. These results demonstrate how frustration can convert a weak thermal perturbation into a large nonlinear electrical response, providing a route to nonlinear magnetotransport in compensated ferrimagnets.

</details>


### [112] [Hyperchaos in a Magnetic Nanodisk Driven by Ferromagnetic Resonance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27884)
*A Kolli,Hugo Merbouche,Salvatore Perna,Claudio Serpico,Damien Rontani,Grégoire de Loubens*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过微磁模拟与时间序列分析，系统研究了垂直磁化纳米圆盘在铁磁共振非线性区的混沌动力学，绘制了从稳定周期轨道到奇异吸引子的拓扑转变图谱，发现该系统在实验可达的控制参数区域可呈现高达三个正李雅普诺夫指数的超混沌行为，并揭示了动力学复杂度与参与自旋波模式数量之间的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索纳米尺度磁性系统中非线性混沌动力学的潜在应用价值，特别是将磁性纳米器件作为非传统信息处理的熵源。尽管磁体中的非线性动力学已被广泛研究，但在纳米限制系统中系统性地刻画超混沌行为并建立其与自旋波模式之间的定量联系仍然缺乏。

Method: 采用大规模微磁模拟与时间序列分析相结合的方法，在控制参数空间中进行系统扫描；使用李雅普诺夫指数量化动力学复杂度以识别超混沌区；运用模式投影技术解析动力学中参与的自旋波量子化模式及其与混沌复杂度的对应关系。

Result: 研究发现即使在简单的纳米尺度磁性系统中，也能在实验可访问的大范围控制参数区域观察到具有最多三个正李雅普诺夫指数的超混沌动力学；通过模式投影技术证明了产生的混沌复杂度与参与动力学的量化自旋波模式数量直接相关。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了磁性纳米自旋电子器件作为多功能熵源用于非常规信息处理的可行性，为基于磁体混沌动力学的类脑计算和随机计算等新范式提供了物理基础。

Abstract: We investigate the chaotic dynamics driven in the nonlinear regime of ferromagnetic resonance of an out-of-plane magnetized nanodisk in detail. By combining extensive micromagnetic simulations with time-series analysis across the control parameter space, we map the topological transitions from stable periodic orbits to strange attractors and quantify the dynamical complexity. Despite the simplicity of our nanoscale system, we evidence that it can exhibit hyperchaotic dynamics with up to three positive Lyapunov exponents in vast regions of the control plane accessible to experimental studies. Using a mode projection technique, we unveil that the generated complexity is related to the number of quantized spin-wave modes participating in the dynamics. Our findings establish magnon-spintronic nanodevices as versatile entropy sources for unconventional processing of information.

</details>

Method: 利用法布里-珀罗干涉作为探测手段，分析大角度扭转双层石墨烯在标称单极性区域中出现的反常电阻振荡。通过分析零场干涉图样确定隐藏腔体的位置和尺寸，并结合磁输运测量区分局域腔模与全局共振（依据二者不同的磁场依赖关系）。最后使用提取的电势分布进行数值模拟以验证模型。

Result: 成功识别出由器件中的无意局部掺杂形成的隐藏腔体，确定了该不均匀性的位置和尺寸；磁输运测量支持该模型，能在磁场上区分局域腔模与全局共振；模拟结果再现了实验特征。

Conclusion: 干涉光谱学是一种简单、非侵入性的探针，可用于识别弹道器件中的局部缺陷和内部电势势垒，为表征器件内部非均匀性提供了新手段。

Abstract: We use Fabry-Pérot interference to probe the internal potential profile of large-angle twisted bilayer graphene. We trace anomalous resistance oscillations in the nominally unipolar regime to a hidden cavity formed by unintentional local doping in our device. By analyzing zero-field interference patterns, we determine the location and size of this inhomogeneity. Magnetotransport measurements support the model, distinguishing local cavity modes from global resonances through their different magnetic dependence. Simulations using our extracted profile reproduce the experimental features. Our results highlight interference spectroscopy as a simple, non-invasive probe for identifying local defects and internal potential barriers in ballistic devices.

</details>


### [114] [An iterative method bridging DFT, disorder averaging, and experiment in intercalated materials: application to Au-intercalated graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28296)
*Poonam Kumari,Alberto Zobelli,Igor de Melo Froldi,Adeline Crepieux,Laurent Simon,Cristina Bena*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合密度泛函理论（DFT）、紧束缚（TB）模型、自洽T矩阵近似（SCTMA）无序平均以及角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）实验的通用迭代方法，用于构建插层体系的等效模型。将该方法应用于Au团簇插层石墨烯体系，成功复现了实验中的主要ARPES特征，包括Van Hove奇点的展宽和色散中的扭结（kink）特征，并识别出关键微观机制为插层物特定轨道与石墨烯态的杂化以及插层诱导的局域散射势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 插层可以强烈地改变主体材料的电子色散，但如何准确构建插层体系的有效模型以理解并复现ARPES实验观测到的电子结构变化是一个挑战。作者希望开发一种通用的、将第一性原理计算与实验数据相结合的迭代方法，从而系统地捕捉插层对宿主材料电子结构的影响。

Method: 提出一种通用迭代方法，流程为：(1) 使用DFT识别插层体系中相关的微观自由度并约束选定的模型参数；(2) 基于DFT结果构建紧束缚（TB）模型；(3) 在TB模型基础上引入无序效应，采用自洽T矩阵近似（SCTMA）进行无序平均；(4) 将SCTMA计算结果与ARPES实验数据对比，根据差异进一步细化模型参数，迭代直至复现实验。

Result: 将该方法应用于Au团簇插层石墨烯体系，成功复现了Au团簇相的主要ARPES特征：Van Hove奇点的展宽以及色散中扭结（kink-like）特征的出现。分析确定了两个关键微观因素：选定的插层物轨道与石墨烯态之间的杂化，以及插层诱导的局域散射势。

Conclusion: 该DFT-TB-SCTMA-实验迭代方法为构建插层体系的有效模型提供了一个通用框架，能够将第一性原理计算与光谱实验相结合，准确描述插层引起的电子结构修饰，并揭示其背后的关键微观物理机制。

Abstract: Intercalation can strongly modify the electronic dispersion of a host material, as directly revealed by angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES). We develop a general iterative method combining density functional theory (DFT), tight-binding (TB), disorder averaging within the self-consistent T-matrix approximation (SCTMA), and experiment, to construct an effective model of the intercalated system. DFT identifies the relevant microscopic degrees of freedom and constrains selected model parameters, while comparison of SCTMA calculations with experiment guides their further refinement. We apply this method to graphene intercalated with Au clusters and show that it reproduces the main ARPES signatures of the Au-cluster phase, including the broadening of the V12an Hove singularity and the emergence of kink-like features in the dispersion. The essential microscopic ingredients identified by the analysis are the hybridization between selected intercalant orbitals and the graphene states, together with an intercalation-induced local scattering potential.

</details>


### [115] [Effects of gold cluster intercalation in graphene: stationary waves and modified QPI features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28297)
*Cristina Bena,Marion Cranney,Poonam Kumari,Alberto Zobelli,Laurent Simon*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了外延石墨烯/SiC上Au插层产生的团簇相，发现其具有集中于布里渊区M点附近的驻波和准粒子干涉（QPI）特征。通过理论模型证明，Au插层于石墨烯空心位下方会在周围六个碳原子上诱发局部散射势，基于T矩阵处理可再现实验中FT-STS观测到的椭圆QPI结构，并解释实空间近驻波的产生机制及LDOS对比的能量依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验中在SiC上外延石墨烯的Au插层体系中观察到了异常的驻波和集中于M点附近的QPI特征，这些现象无法用传统散射机制直观解释。作者旨在从理论上阐明这种非寻常QPI特征和驻波现象的物理起源，揭示Au插层态的微观散射机制。

Method: 建立Au插层于石墨烯空心位下方的模型，该插层原子对周围六个碳原子（环形散射体）产生局域散射势。采用T矩阵方法处理该环状散射体的散射问题，计算QPI结构；同时计算实空间驻波图样以及团簇上/外的局域态密度（LDOS）对比。

Result: T矩阵计算再现了以M点为中心的椭圆QPI结构，与实验FT-STS一致；这些QPI特征自然产生了实空间中近似静止的驻波图样；团簇上和外的LDOS对比的符号和幅度具有强能量依赖性，与实验观测吻合。

Conclusion: Au插层于石墨烯空心位下方这一简单的局域散射模型能够全面解释实验中观察到的M点附近椭圆QPI、准静止驻波以及LDOS对比的能量依赖行为，为理解石墨烯插层体系中非传统散射特征提供了统一的理论框架。

Abstract: Gold intercalation beneath epitaxial graphene on SiC produces a cluster phase with unusual standing waves and quasiparticle-interference (QPI) features concentrated near the graphene M points. We show that this can be explained by Au intercalation below graphene hollow sites, which induces a local scattering potential on the six surrounding carbon atoms. Within a T-matrix treatment, this ring-like scatterer produces elliptical QPI structures centered near M, in agreement with the experimental FT-STS measurements. We further show that these QPI features naturally generate the nearly stationary standing-wave patterns observed in real space. Finally, we compute the local-density-of-states contrast on and off a small cluster and show that its sign and magnitude are strongly energy dependent, consistent with the experimental observations.

</details>


### [116] [Current-based RF charge sensing in a carbon nanotube](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28313)
*Marta Cagetti,Stefan Forstner,Victor Champain,Roger Tormo-Queralt,Christoffer B. Møller,Sergio L. De Bonis,Chandan Samanta,Elsa Vázquez-Rodriguez,Eneko Mateos-Madinabeitia,David A. Czaplewski,Adrian Bachtold*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超灵敏电荷检测方法要么需要阻抗匹配的谐振电路，要么需要毫米级放大器与样品的近距离，这些都增加了系统复杂性并限制了器件设计自由度。作者旨在提出一种更简便的电荷传感方案，以克服这些限制并提升检测性能。

Method: 提出一种在悬浮碳纳米管中实现的电流模式电荷传感器，利用RLC谐振电路在1.25 MHz谐振频率下工作，实现对电荷的灵敏检测。该传感器被用于测量同一纳米管中静电定义的双量子点的电荷态。

Result: 电荷灵敏度达到0.15 μe/√Hz；在3.56 μs积分时间内实现双量子点电荷态的单次读出，10^7次测量中无误判，信噪比为17，优于当前最先进水平。

Conclusion: 该电流模式电荷传感器在无需阻抗匹配谐振电路和近距离放大器的情况下，实现了高性能的电荷检测与单次读出，为量子信息处理和凝聚态物理研究提供了更简洁高效的探测方案。

Abstract: Ultra-sensitive charge detection is a widely used tool for quantum electronics with applications in quantum information processing and in probing the physics of condensed matter systems. Existing approaches require either an impedance-matched resonant circuit, or millimeter-scale proximity between amplifier and sample, both adding complexity and constraining device design. In this work, we introduce a current-mode charge sensor in a suspended carbon nanotube, operating at the $1.25$ MHz resonance of an RLC tank circuit and achieving a charge sensitivity of $0.15~μe/\sqrt{\mathrm{Hz}}$. We utilize it to measure a double quantum dot (DQD) electrostatically defined in the same nanotube, revealing a highly regular charge stability diagram. We perform single-shot readout of the DQD charge state at an integration time of $3.56~μ\mathrm{s}$, without any false assignments over $10^{7}$ measurements and a signal-to-noise ratio of 17 exceeding the state of the art.

</details>


### [117] [Anomalous metal and superconducting phases in rhombohedral graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28425)
*Anna Okounkova,Abigail Sohm,Tobias Faehndrich,Manish Kumar,Derek Waleffe,Jiaqiang Yan,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Joshua Folk,Matthew Yankowitz*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了菱面体石墨烯/WSe₂异质结中栅压调控的超导相，发现零电阻超导态与有限饱和电阻态共存于栅压空间中的孤立口袋区域。有限电阻态在面内磁场下扩展合并，表现出一系列与"异常金属"态吻合的现象学特征，该状态在薄膜超导体中已观测数十年但缺乏理论解释。超清洁菱面体石墨烯的可调性和可重复性对认为该行为源于外在因素的解释构成强约束，为该现象提供了新的研究平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 石墨烯基体系中已建立二维超导性并普遍存在非传统配对的证据，但在多个栅调控相中，电阻在降温时并不消失而饱和于有限值，这一反常行为缺乏系统理解。作者旨在通过超洁净的菱面体石墨烯/WSe₂体系对这种现象进行系统性研究，以厘清其起源并约束外在解释。

Method: 在菱面体石墨烯/WSe₂衬底上进行系统性栅压-温度-磁场输运测量。零磁场下扫描栅压空间，识别零电阻超导与有限饱和电阻两种区域；在基温（毫开尔文）下对温度、垂直磁场和电流的临界值进行精细调控，并施加小面内磁场观察口袋的演化和合并行为。

Result: 发现栅压空间中存在两种截然不同的区域：零电阻超导区域和有限饱和电阻区域，二者对应独立的孤立口袋。零磁场下两种状态的转变均表现为随温度、垂直磁场和电流超过临界值后突然跳变至正常态；面内磁场可扩展并使口袋合并，最终在基温下形成零电阻与有限电阻之间的尖锐边界。有限电阻态重现了"异常金属"的关键现象学特征。

Conclusion: 超洁净菱面体石墨烯体系中观测到的有限饱和电阻态与"异常金属"现象一致，且体系的高度可调性和可重复性显著排除了外在因素（如杂质或非均匀性）作为该现象起源的可能性，为理解和最终解释这一长期悬而未决的超导反常金属态提供了理想的新平台。

Abstract: Two-dimensional superconductivity is now well established in graphene-based systems, with many such realizations showing evidence for unconventional pairing. Yet in several of the gate-tuned phases that otherwise exhibit clear signatures of superconductivity, the resistance does not vanish as temperature is lowered, instead saturating at a finite value. Here we report a systematic study of this behavior in rhombohedral graphene on a WSe$_2$ substrate, finding regions of gate space with zero-resistance superconductivity alongside others with finite saturation resistance. At zero magnetic field, these regions appear as isolated pockets in gate space that otherwise exhibit strikingly similar phenomenology, including abrupt transitions to the normal state as temperature, perpendicular magnetic field, and current are raised above critical values. A small in-plane field expands and merges these pockets without qualitatively altering their behavior, producing a sharp boundary at millikelvin base temperature between states of zero or finite resistance. The finite-resistance state reproduces key phenomenology associated with the anomalous metal, a state that has been observed in thin-film superconductors for decades but lacks an accepted theoretical explanation. The tunability and reproducibility of ultra-clean rhombohedral graphene place strong constraints on extrinsic explanations and provide a new platform for understanding this behavior.

</details>


### [118] [Observation of long-lived spin order in nanoconfined water](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28480)
*Rohma Khan,Kang Xu,Nathaniel Jeffries,Ankit Bhardwaj,Boya Radha,Daniela Pagliero,Carlos A. Meriles*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文利用氮-空位（NV）中心核磁共振（NMR）波谱技术，在5.6纳米通道中探测温度依赖的水分子动力学。系统在整个研究温度范围内保持液态，且扩散率显著被抑制，从而可以直接检测1H NMR谱。偶尔质子共振会转化为具有数十kHz分裂的双峰，作者将其归因于由激光照射期间光注入的溶剂化电子所引发的长寿命顺磁电荷复合物介导的超精细相互作用。这种特征的间歇性出现暗示了由贯穿受限液体的电荷水合复合物相关群体组成的亚稳态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米尺度受限液体的动力学行为与体相液体存在显著差异，但在受控条件下研究其动力学在实验上极具挑战。作者旨在通过NV中心NMR技术直接探测纳米受限水的核磁共振谱及其动力学特征。

Method: 使用氮-空位（NV）中心核磁共振（NMR）波谱技术，对5.6纳米通道中受限的水进行温度依赖的谱学测量。通过系统在整个温度范围内保持液态且扩散率被抑制的特性，实现了1H NMR谱的直接检测。实验中激光照射用于光学注入电子，从而研究顺磁电荷复合物对质子共振的影响。

Result: 系统在整个研究温度范围内保持液态，表现出强烈抑制的扩散率，并能直接检测到1H NMR谱。质子共振偶尔转化为具有数十kHz分裂的双峰，该特征归因于长寿命顺磁电荷复合物介导的超精细相互作用，这些复合物由激光照射期间光注入的溶剂化电子所引发。

Conclusion: 双峰特征的间歇性出现表明存在一种亚稳态，该亚稳态包含贯穿整个受限液体的相关电荷-水合复合物群体，为纳米尺度受限水中的电荷介导动力学提供了新的见解。

Abstract: Liquids confined to nanometer-scale geometries exhibit behavior that departs markedly from their bulk counterparts, yet studying their dynamics under controlled conditions remains experimentally challenging. Here, we use nitrogen-vacancy (NV) center nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR) spectroscopy to probe water confined in 5.6 nm channels as a function of temperature. The system remains liquid throughout the investigated temperature range and exhibits strongly suppressed diffusivity, enabling direct detection of its 1H NMR spectrum. Occasionally, the proton resonance transforms into a doublet with a splitting of several tens of kilohertz, which we tentatively attribute to hyperfine interactions mediated by long-lived paramagnetic charge complexes, in turn seeded by solvated electrons optically injected during laser illumination. The intermittent appearance of this feature suggests a metastable state comprising a correlated population of charge-hydration complexes extending throughout the confined liquid.

</details>
